### Implementation order and current source map

1. **Scope minimum.** In `scope-gates.jq:875–892`, retain the unseeded/missing-family
   branch and the existing checks for zero cases, failed, inconclusive and
   passed != total. Add the `multi` minimum check to the failing branch: when
   `trial_policy.kind == "multi"` and total < `minimum_trials`, emit the existing
   `scope.eval-failing`. A `single` family still needs at least one case and all
   passed. No dashboard shape, reason id, qualification or enablement rule changes.
2. **Program uniqueness.** Extend `evals.jq:1008–1016` `dashboard_results_ok` to
   require that the flattened `.body.cases[]` pairs `[.family_id,.case_id]` are
   unique. Compare pair arrays, not concatenated strings. The existing
   `build-dashboard` and `validate-dashboard` entries (`:1033–1052`) then reject
   duplicates as `E_SHAPE` and boolean false. Retain result-digest uniqueness,
   catalog/evaluator validation and canonical dashboard reconstruction. Do not
   alter `build_dashboard`'s shape, totals or family policy copy (`:901–954`).
3. **Driver error boundary.** In `evals-driver.sh:187–248` `run_dashboard`, keep the
   pair replay and byte comparison through `:236` unchanged. After collecting the
   result digests at `:239`, reject duplicate result digests as today's `E_RUNTIME`
   before checking family/case duplicates. For distinct results, check the same
   pair uniqueness over validated `results.jsonl`; false emits `E_SHAPE`, while
   a jq execution failure remains `E_RUNTIME`. Keep all earlier parse, shape,
   canonical and relation errors, including forged-result `E_RELATION`. Do not
   turn every failed `build-dashboard` into `E_SHAPE`.
4. **Pins.** Compute the final program SHA-256 and update driver `:75` and launcher
   `:148`. Then compute the final driver digest and update launcher `:150`.
   The base program digest is
   `f5eee09d02d54934cf9313f5a9e2731dbfe6c4e83b4f5f49eaa5073a19040cd7`;
   the base driver digest is
   `5bc88cd11185a4fb3029ee3cb9b746b588c965ab5b7fb15507431d8eb499beb6`.
   A repository digest search finds these three references only outside `work/`.
   The current scope program digest has no shipped pin. Do not add a closure pin.
   Re-run the search at implementation base; an extra moved pin outside these
   allowed paths stops for a plan amendment.
5. **Tests below.** All fixtures live in each existing test's temporary directory.
   Add no committed seed, approval, fixture scope or fake native receipt. Keep all
   existing assertions and the normal full-test entry points.
6. **Docs.** In `docs/components.md` under `Inactive eval and trace framework`
   (`:820`, catalog count at `:830–837`), and `RESTORE.md`'s eval explanation
   (`:1071`), describe the minimum and duplicate-pair behavior. State seven seeded,
   two declared; sources/relay/replay, real seeds and operator labels remain due.
   Do not describe later sources as shipped.

| --- | --- | --- |
| M4 cross-result repeat | Dashboard test's shipped core result plus its no-newline/earlier-time result (`:120–123`), through `run-evals.sh dashboard` | Distinct digests verified; `E_SHAPE`, nonzero exit, no stdout |
| Original duplicate input | Keep dashboard test `duplicate-result` (`:158–159`) | Same pair twice remains `E_RUNTIME` |

| Direct program rejection | Feed the cross-result repeat to the shipped jq program's `build-dashboard` and `validate-dashboard` | Build errors with `E_SHAPE` and no JSON; validate returns exactly false |
| Direct program acceptance | Feed the corresponding no-repeat fixtures to both direct entries | Build succeeds; validate true on its canonical output |
| M2/M3 below floor | Temporary scope-test dashboard: `stale-moved-artifacts` uses multi/minimum 3, total 2, all passed | Exact reasons `["scope.eval-failing","scope.sandbox-receipt-missing"]`; proposal absent |

| M2/M3 failed/inconclusive | Total 3, with one failed, then one inconclusive; remaining passed | Exact reasons `["scope.eval-failing","scope.sandbox-receipt-missing"]`; proposal absent |
| Single regression | Same required seeded family, single/minimum 1: zero; one passed; one failed; one inconclusive | Zero/failed/inconclusive have the same exact two reasons; one passed has only the baseline receipt refusal |
| Declared regression | Require malicious family from the unchanged temporary dashboard | Exact reasons `["scope.eval-family-unseeded","scope.sandbox-receipt-missing"]`; proposal absent |
| Catalog boundary | Fixed digest and both declared families checked in dashboard test; retain existing seven-set aggregate and framework catalog assertions | Original bytes, 7 seeded / 2 declared, unchanged 138-case aggregate |

   fixtures and their digest lists now; both fields are required in the final PR.
   Bind their real evaluator content SHA as specified below; a placeholder SHA
   no longer satisfies the scope contract.
2. **Review records.** Implement the R8.3 review case and shared shapes described
   in the final-delivery section below. Pin a single representation for R7.1:
   `labels` is a schema-1 `eval_labels` envelope with id
   `evals.reviewer-severity.labels.v1`, body exactly `{trials}`. Each entry is
   exactly `{comment_id,verdict_sha256,findings,missed_important,grade_status}`;
   findings are `{index,tag,label}` and missed entries `{summary,evidence_link}`.
   Use positive decimal-string comment ids consistently in labels, approval,
   verdict filename and `review.<id>`; require distinct ids in sorted order.
   Finding indexes are distinct positive integers, tags P0–P3, labels R7.1's three
   values; missed summaries are one line, at most 280 characters. Grade status is
   passed/failed only. Validate field types, exact keys and existing parsed limits.
   This stores operator decisions; it does not assign labels or infer grades.
   Validate approval's exact schema-1 envelope/id and the nine R7.2 body fields,
   family id, approver `yihanzhu`, timestamp and distinct sorted selection. An extra
   field or caller approval property is invalid. No approval is shipped now.
3. **Program result path.** Extend `expectation_shape`, `seed_set_shape` and
   `observation_shape` only for reviews. Add `$source` through `grade`,
   `grader_kind_for`, `trace_event` and their callers. R8.4 passes through status
   with the existing reason vocabulary specified below; other human/model families
   retain `evals.no-deterministic-grader`. Require `human` in review cases and trace,
   and refuse it for the other sources. Tool ref is `eval-record-evaluator.v1`,

   existing source branches and direct jq argument interface unchanged.
4. **Hold files once.** Implement the final section's `stage_expected` rule now for
   record closure and review files, after existing closure loops. Reuse the schema

   review `shared.files` entry from the repository's fixed verdict prefix, never

   Resolve reuse consistently across dashboard pairs; two digests for one path
   refuse `E_RELATION`. A missing or changed verdict is `E_RELATION`; a closure
   pin mismatch is `E_STALE`. Retain the current snapshot byte limits and private
   modes. Stage canonical bundle/labels content pairs in private work, recompute
   their LF-terminated hashes and compare the supplied digests before use.
5. **Fixed approval.** Add `review_approval_sha256=none` in the driver and matching
   `review_approval_sha=none` in the launcher. Implement the complete conditional

   copy; with a digest, only the shipped

6. **Transcription and M5.** Add an explicit reviews arm to both `produce_result`

**Record raw events and preserve the real recheck result.** Keep a local boundary
for each private recheck within the same disposition. Record its events in order

| Actual path | Outward result and identity obligation |
| --- | --- |
| Bare refresh: unmarked OSError A, reporting MemoryError B, successful cleanup | A remains primary absent an earlier independent error. B stays visible. There is no recheck conversion here. |
| Recheck: A then unmarked B, original guard converts A | With no earlier independent error or control, the recheck raises the actual R at that guard with its original code (E_STORE at final C1, the supplied code at acquisition, E_STALE at the layout root). Preserve from None, R.context is A, and B reachable. C1 reports R at its original site once. |
| An independent ordinary E already preceded this recheck | E keeps its earlier position. Retain the recheck's actual outward failure, A and every distinct report error. Within the recheck group, R represents its triggering A at A's position for ordinary selection. A previously captured in an independent operation keeps that earlier occurrence. |
| A control occurs before, inside or after this recheck | Compare all actual KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit and marked objects across the operation in original event order. The earliest wins, even when it is a report or conversion failure. A later control remains visible. Supply the original outward failure to the original C1 report, independent of any earlier overall winner. If the original guard was bypassed, produce no R. Retain an R only if actually constructed and raised. |
| Original Refusal construction itself fails with F | There is no R. The actual failure F represents this failed conversion at its business position. Preserve F, its original links to the triggering error and all other distinct errors. An earlier independent ordinary still precedes it and the earliest actual control still wins. C1 sees the actual failure from the check. Never retry conversion. |

Forwarding a grouped recheck result or a prior child result does not observe a

business semantics; no-argument Reader.close does not import the caller's error.

**One caller record for an ordinary operation.** Main makes one private
disposition before installing the four original handlers, constructing

or Reader constructor does not return. Its explicit ordinary argument flows
through the Reader, _ReadOnlyGit and admitted child calls. A child takes the

Preserve copy-close versus held-close accounting and the zero-component

Use one activation per call that can produce a new entry. Its fixed ordinary

**Receipt, order and forwarding.** The callback's actual final raise is the

A built error in the outer marker assignment has not reached that raise.

At the real protected call, a passive exit receives incoming as a formal

Read the history only from a caller-owned root or a nominated fixed

The caller retains the root through cleanup and through its actual receipt.
It can retrieve secondaries there even if the Reader never returned. Remove

The installed handler surrounds that real call. Its caught raw argument

Only a confirmed ordinary operation can enter terminal delivery. The caller

**Reserve the actual raw call.** Prepare one exact private root slot current_raw
with an idle sentinel distinct from the initial-head sentinel. Do this before

This reads one existing reference, no frame or locals. Prebind it and the

The installed handler's outer catch must already cover loading inherited
from current_raw, publishing K to that slot and the direct call of the raw

| Interruption point | Which pending receipt is authoritative |
| --- | --- |
| Before K is published | The previous slot still describes the predecessors. No raw call for this activation has begun. Its covering catch can receive a new outward without inventing a constructed object. |
| After publication, before raw or during its construction/marker | With no actual crossing of that interval, an inner callback must bare-raise its own S/F to the live covering caller. If raw has begun, its actual local constructor/marker receives that error first. If raw has not begun, the installed catch receives it and never starts that skipped call. |
| Original throw leaves raw, or a new throw aborts after publication but before raw | The actual throwable's traceback crosses this owner's exact protected raw-call or covered post-publication delivery site. Its frame, fixed root, K, inherited, code/globals and instruction range authenticate discharge. This witness already exists when control reaches the catch, before its first classifier or body store. A later callback may use it even if it interrupts that catch. A pre-publication site alone cannot discharge K. |
| Retirement and nested return | Leave the last published key strongly rooted. Never clear it in a vulnerable finally or write inherited back over a new callback. To resolve *this callback's inherited key*, use the saved entry prefix. A certified discharged owner leads to that owner's inherited key. The idle sentinel ends the chain. A complete prefix with a claimed generation still awaiting its crossing routes the raw outward to its existing cover. Stop only on true eligibility, live cover or a genuine evidence failure. A partial read cannot prove absence. |

If K equals inherited, this activation is a guest of the same generation.
Its crossing cannot retire the claiming activation. If K differs, publishing
it claims a new generation and retains its parent in inherited. Before a
new independent raw call can complete, the real S/F must add a fresh thrown
object or a fresh original traceback node in its own activation, even when F
is the same object as before. A pure relay never rewinds latest to an older
head. Keep the saved node itself if that object's traceback later grows.
Overlapping entries with the same unchanged head can share one claimant.
If X claims the key before Y's pending store, X always throws, so Y cannot
resume to claim that key too. Only a crossing of the claiming generation
can retire it. Check these rules also for a precommitted, unraised built
object and a repeated constructor F. Missing VM nodes give no discharge.

In the disputed pair, X directly in the terminal selector inherits a
discharged generation with the certified S1-to-incoming-Q crossing. X in
Y's precommit marker inherits Y's newly published generation. S1 is its
*entry* evidence; it cannot be Y's later raw crossing. Although X.previous
is S1 in both runs, only the first can normalize to Q now. The second sends
actual X to Y. Once X has crossed the protected call in Y, a callback in

| Actual boundary | What may leave it |
| --- | --- |
| Unconfirmed fd6, gate, writer, or normal business | Original raw delivery. The fd6 OSError handler bare-raises the exact marked InterruptedError and only a genuine probe failure selects its old fallback. Default calls retain their argument and error order. |
| Three private rechecks, C1 and owner retirement | The raw A, B and conversion R/F run through their original guards. Preserve R.context=A and from None. C1-final receives the local R/F. C1-close receives close A, or B only when close succeeded. No terminal winner replaces any of these inputs. Reader finishes source-copy, reverse-owned and completion order. Main still finishes its original FIFO exit. An internal Reader outcome returned to that main is not a final operation exit. |
| Nested callback construction or marker | Resolve the saved inherited reservation before selecting Q. A live predecessor gets the real raw S/F at its original local catch. Each actual throw counts once. |
| Terminal receipt still unwinding Q | Later objects go to the real root. If the receiver completes and returns false, the interpreter continues that original Q. If ordinary A is pending and a genuinely earlier eligible control is absent, the first real S wins. A false return for A is not a substitute for sending S. |
| Terminal receipt interrupted | Use the actual previous/during edges of each real throw, not a queue. The terminal handler may deliver an earlier Q only on the positive witnesses below. If the current object is the winner, bare-raise it from its genuine handler. Otherwise call the bound relay on the exact Q. The callback's original S/F remains accessible. |

For the two interruptions, take raw Q=SystemExit or KeyboardInterrupt
escaping the final original owner into its receiving exit. S1 interrupts
before Q is pinned. If S1 cannot yet see an earlier authenticated Q, it
bare-raises S1 to the already established terminal catch surrounding that

throw/crossing. The catch examines this fixed prefix. If S2 interrupts this

S2's actual handler can therefore elect Q itself and call the relay.
It does not return to finish S1's failed lookup or require Q to be active.
Earlier terminal selectors seeing that same certified relay result let it
propagate. They must not reclassify Q as a new failed read, constructor,
C1 report or raw callback merely because it interrupted their own code.

If S3 interrupts that terminal selection or the relay's Python entry,
its own genuine activation has the retained S2/S1 prefix and selects in

outer holder must first receive its true S/F as in the table. Each descent
requires a distinct actual callback. For a finite delivery sequence, the

and emits the same earliest Q. It never creates another job to publish its
own failure. No failed business operation, close, failed frame
materialization or fallible marker getter is retried. Distinguish fresh
reads by a different real activation from retrying a failed operation.
A predecessor cannot become eligible by being constructed or merely
written to latest. A matching actual throw and protected crossing are
required. Never infer a candidate from an old ambient context.

This proof has a boundary. At every actual dispatch before a witness exists,
the exact raw return must still have a live enclosing receipt. After that
last receipt is crossed, a later callback must be able to find the witness
in its own saved prefix before relying on it. Verify both sides at actual
method entry, nested raw exit, owner crossing and callback delivery to the
selector; a Python try, store or getter is not an atomic interval. If the
normal runtime can displace Q before the registered receiving activation
exists and leave no other authenticated Q or covering catch, stop on that
normal gap. Do not report optional incomplete access as successful election.
Do not change an original local catch, install a signal mask or inspect
the callback's interrupted-frame argument to bridge it.

**Transfer of the selected instance.** The proposed private relay is one
already exhausted or explicitly closed empty generator with its builtin

callback until the caller receives the real outward.

Closed-generator transfer is a specific, unverified premise. Bind the

| Ordinary main branch | Existing receiver and exit |
| --- | --- |
| No Reader yet | Keep the root across handler installation, _ControlFIFO construction, fd6 and Reader construction. Preserve the installed signal's original timing. No close of a nonexistent Reader, FIFO or gate session. A failed Reader constructor drains its actual partial owners once. Main still receives its error and owns any successfully returned FIFO for the original finally. |

**Separate FIFO finding.** At r5 Reader 3110–3119 _ControlFIFO.close clears
both FD attributes, attempts ready_fd, conditionally attempts fd and finally
closes the parent. If the ready close raises, fd is never attempted and its
attribute is already empty. This is an open P1 on that pre-existing owner.
This plan covers its outward result at main. It does not change FIFO's body
or claim complete FIFO drain. End-to-end all-owner/no-leak proof, dependent
native qualification and G3 remain blocked on its distinct repair and proof.
If independent review finds the new ordinary handoff itself needs that
internal repair, stop at the affected gate before source work.

**Live references and proof gates.** Count actual owners and active calls.
One pending FD plus the original list suffix suffices to identify an
acquisition's resources. One active close consumes one component. A
zero-component owner performs no final C1 check and must still pass its
pre-existing error to the parent. A parent and child share the same head.
Retiring a drained owner removes its resource from the original list without
clearing reachable history. An undrained or post-close-failed opaque owner
keeps its original ownership rule. No selection, frame read or second
allocation to append the failed append may be needed to drain a real FD.
Prepare fixed receiver methods and owner locals before the first acquisition.

At root zero there is one caller disposition and no Reader-owned descriptor.
Phase one has one root dirfd. Phase two holds a root and at most the current
child, each with its real component list. A candidate copy owns its parent
and the actual live writer/readback (a successful writer close precedes

Reader.close there is one reverse-list cursor and one current owner. A C1

at its actual site. The two work objects may be the same. Count each real

For a concrete execution count P actual registered producer activations, C
actual callback activations and T real traceback nodes retained by their

has at most its listed fixed local references, each callback its fixed

that storage without evidence. For a frozen inspection prefix its work and

and child frames, and post-return roots. Count actual fresh callbacks without

bound on event frames. Materialization, frame references pinning locals
and their transitive data, callbacks, exception creation, reception, selection,

An actual lost frame requires a lawful outlet and a return to the affected

#### Aggregate namespace compatibility

Use one private classifier in qualify.py, called only by aggregate for each
configuration's existing store check. It receives the already validated config,
that store's original named receipt-attempt set, and a separate companion-eligible
set derived from successfully revalidated batch/case/attempt relationships.
Keep the original receipt set and its missing/duplicate/rerun checks. Add companion
eligibility only for a case in a first received batch whose batch bindings and
verify_case have both passed against prepare's request and that configuration.
Never derive an eligible name from an entry prefix, a claimed case field, another
store or an unverified cached record.

For each eligible attempt, generate exactly `.capture-` + attempt_id + `.pending`
and `.capture-` + attempt_id. The latter is the published name; no third suffix
is accepted. Require the bound principal UID to equal the actual UID. Require the configured path to remain physical and absolute. Hold its path
components using directory-fd relative O_DIRECTORY|O_NOFOLLOW opens, retaining
named/held identity checks through the store check. Apply the existing host
store_root_state_ok mechanism to the final held store fd, as check_store_root
does. Keep original ancestor constraints and root empty ACL/nonlink/uid/gid/mode
rules; do not follow a symlink through an intermediate component. Take the
complete fd-based directory inventory. Every entry must be an original named
receipt or one exact eligible companion; unknown entries retain
qualification.batch-rerun. Both companion names present for one attempt also
refuse as batch-rerun. A companion never counts as a receipt.

For each present companion, compare no-follow named metadata, an open relative to
that held root using O_DIRECTORY|O_NOFOLLOW, and fstat identity. Reuse
store_root_state_ok for directory type, principal UID, consumer GID, mode 0750
and empty ACL. Use the host's full capture-identity tuple for before/after checks.
Keep the opened directory through the check; recheck named identity, held identity
and metadata before closing. Recheck the complete root inventory, its named/held
identity and original root metadata after all entries. Reuse host metadata/ACL
primitives; add no source module or public API. Metadata/open/recheck/close failure
makes aggregate incomplete with its existing binding-mismatch reason. Preserve
the first failure; cancellation propagates after cleanup. Close each acquired fd
once in reverse order, including partial acquisition and failure paths. A close
error cannot result in a successful classification; do not retry an uncertain fd.

A correctly bound 0750 pending directory can be a partial publication. A pending
directory left at mkdir's 0700 after failed chown/chmod is refused. Published
without complete.json, or while .completion.pending exists, can also be partial:
host renames the directory before publishing completion. This classifier checks
the namespace and directory custody, not inner capture payload/completion validity.

| Projection/metadata formatting or second-line write failure | Original first-line code and failure stand; metadata absent/UNKNOWN, no retry |
| Existing success path or cancellation | Success remains silent on stderr with unchanged stdout; cancellation preserves its original code, wait/close/trap behavior and failure |

Before adoption, verify D's rows against its exact tree and B's rows against B.
Before each source checkpoint, measure all 84 final files as
`{path,mode,type,git_blob,sha256,size_bytes,readers}`. Final hashes cannot exist in
this pre-code plan: the manager must receive the complete measured receipt from
the clean final code head, independently review it and bind it to that head before
source acceptance. Missing/new/duplicate/extra path, wrong file type or unreadable
bytes stops; a new member returns to G2. The three new files must be real connected
owners before any receipt can be complete. Repeat the receipt after every changed
source/policy byte and after the final main merge. Never reuse a prior receipt.

Keep source pins in existing eval program/driver/launcher tables, not a second
shipped registry. Pin leaf source/policy files first, then program SHA in driver
and launcher, then final driver SHA in launcher. The coordinator cannot contain
its own digest or a copy of this closure list: it consumes only the already-held
fixed closure supplied by the private driver. Search old/new digest occurrences
outside work/ and reconcile every actual consumer. A moved pin outside editable
paths pauses for the appropriate artifact gate; it is not permission to bypass it.

Freeze `scripts/core-contract.sh` and all tracked core/v1 and core/v2 files by
B's complete `git ls-tree -r` mode/type/blob table. Verify equality after every
merge and before publication; source adoption contains the same selected exports.
No copied corrective-generation literal enters tests, docs or new code. Resolve G
from the pinned registry and check the unique expected schema path in core_closure.
The unchanged record evaluator/run/schema remain the three-member record closure.

Read edges are finite: writer holds its fixed source list and seven core members;
its validators add the fixed materializer, trace and maintenance files. Generator
holds the exact control/scope policy tree and its reader; reader holds consumer,
writer, preparation, enforcement and source-normalizer files. Host/guest/runtime
and native/helper/verifier build-provenance edges add only the R4A.D rows. The table
records each existing edge. Inspect all fixed open/import/include/build-source
sites in these owners at final head; do not scan runtime/system directories.
The generator's control/scope directory scan must equal this fixed table. A new
file may not enter because it merely appears in a directory.

Build one sorted physical union of existing core/control/orchestrator/adapter/
record/shadow closures. Preserve each logical list. For each duplicate path,
require equal mode/type/size/SHA and bytes, then reuse its one held snapshot.
Current overlap names are mechanically the intersection of the bound lists;
record that exact intersection and count in the final receipt. Do not retain the
old provisional 23/47 counts. Preserve old staging order so tamper precedence
still targets the same earlier file. Snapshot new members only after those loops.

Extend the existing bounded snapshot path only for the closed R4A input classes.
The launcher chooses the byte ceiling from the validated fixed role, not a caller
limit: source 16 MiB/file and 32 MiB total; non-export raw observations 16 MiB
with four 4 MiB partitions; full exports and unified raw use the ledger below;
qualified slots use the RAW16 per-case/window and cumulative limits below. Keep
existing lower per-role limits for all other files. The same regular-file,
non-symlink-parent, descriptor hold, exclusive creation and final recheck apply.
Count bytes actually copied and reject before the next chunk crosses either the
file or aggregate ceiling; stated size/hash cannot reserve free capacity. Record
physical-copy counters separately from each logical closure/input reference.
Never execute a copied qualified slot; only the fixed existing development tools
execute the pinned replay sources. No external helper executable is accepted.

Archived catalog and seed-set are exactly the two R5 files. Qualified byte-only
runtime slots, raw observations and ordinary trial inputs remain separate classes.
No executable/policy is moved into them. Enforce all accepted R4A bounds before
allocation/write, count repeated input and physical storage, and stream qualified
bytes in at most 64 KiB chunks. RAW16 replaces the old aggregate 4 GiB/64-slot
and two-copy 8 GiB boundary with the exact case/window contract below. These are
inactive storage/work limits, not permission to inspect or retain a real runtime.

### Shared interfaces and implementation order

The names below are private functions in the listed existing owners. They take
closed bytes/values, return bytes/values and never accept callbacks, process
objects, arbitrary modules or a general transport. The real caller retains its
current acquisition and authority objects. The offline caller cannot construct
those objects. Change imports only to make value definitions load without entering
`main`; do not relax standalone startup admission or expose a public replay flag.

| Order / owner | Private value boundary | Both required callers and output |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 `runtime-vfkit.py` | `_argv_values(start_raw,config_raw)`; `_state_values(status,body,error)`; `_stop_values(status,body,error)` | Real cmd_argv/state/stop retain reads/REST; coordinator calls values only. Return exact stdout/stderr/exit classification. Keep every existing state name, malformed parse and stop-acceptance branch. |
| 2 guest `observation-values.c/.h` | Typed setup/counter/report functions plus `ys_controller_step(state,event)` for the closed guest event tags | Supervisor calls before each existing decision/action; fixed offline helper feeds historical events. Returned next-action enum is guest-specific, never executable dispatch. Real controller keeps deadline-first order and real ops; offline has no ops table or syscall. |
| 3 verifier.c | Shared instruction/file-byte validation and payload builder; fixed value-only build form | Actual verifier main and coordinator's bounded stdin helper call it. No selected root, candidate executable or alternative checker. |
| 4 host-supervisor.py | `_host_step(state,primitive)`; existing build_limit_rows/build_receipt/derive_outcome receive the recomputed values | Real run_vm performs old driver/process actions; coordinator invokes the same transitions and output owners. CPU/wall none and failure.enforcement-unavailable remain literal. |
| 5 enforced-reproduction.py | `_launch_values(request_raw,incident_raw,evaluation_raw,observation_raw,record_raw,manifest_raw,instruction,control_values,entropy)` | make_launch obtains fresh entropy/held control then calls it; replay supplies historical values. Return exact launch/expectation bytes. No historical values enter `_launch`. |
| 6 preparation/prepare-candidate.py | Separate manifest_for/make_record/producer_identity value construction from reads; typed held-Git inventory, storage observations and runtime bytes | Real prepare/inspect acquire and check the old facts; replay uses identical constructors after rebuilding Git. No producer override or new public operation. |
| 7 shadow/_consumer.py | `_origin_values(files,storage_rows,identity_context,source_bytes)` and existing metadata/ACL predicates | read_store_attempt, held recheck and coverage reader call after their actual holds; replay obtains historical values only. Return canonical origin, never Anchor/HeldPath/session/native release. |
| 8 coverage-source.py | Reuse captured/normalize/kill_normalize; extract existing source identity and receipt construction as `_source_values(request_raw,configuration_bytes,pages,primitive_values)` | Real host source path retains collection/reservation/store checks; coordinator consumes fixed historical pages. No collector or request transport is added. |
| 9 write-shadow.py | Extract _publisher/_admit/_outer_record/provenance/command value construction from acquisition | Existing prepare/write call the same functions with held facts; coordinator uses reconstructed inputs. All old predicates remain; values cannot grant real write admission. |
| 10 scope reader/generator | Extract request/expectation, bundle fact, measured observation, project_register, origins and manifest/completion value constructors | Existing _Reader/_Generator acquire real facts and session checks; coordinator calls values plus real jq evaluators. Public evaluate-scope retains its real reader handshake. |
| 11 replay-malicious.py | One fixed `replay_case(held_case,runtime_root,private_work)` orchestration and comparison | Only the fixed eval-driver arm calls it; no public toolkit, plugin dispatch, transport or arbitrary callable. Expected outputs/invariants go only to final comparison. |

In write-shadow.py's _admit, pass the same materialized.write_set already used
by _admission_values to _candidate_facts. Do not substitute write["write_set"] or
skip candidate validation. Preserve all admission predicates, receipt checks,
Git inventory/commit/tree/parent relations and every full blob/byte/SHA check.
Keep provenance, outer record, seal/close and publication withheld. The existing
shadow-write positive calls and all negative assertions remain the proof;
no copied test or additional invocation is needed.

Instantiate these same owners independently for each applicable R4A.B occurrence.
Keep both launch controllers, all four preparation calls and the two gate reader
contexts separate. Each original request, sequence and output belongs to its
actual caller; the table is not a single-case observation object. Use production
functions for validation and construction, not copied predicates.
For C, compile the same observation-values source for the guest and for its one
value-only helper. `build-guest.py` links it into the existing guest build. Give
verifier.c one fixed value-only main under a compile-time flag, using the same
builder as its normal main; normal native entry/flags remain unchanged. The
existing guest harness tests the shared library. CI's existing Linux compiler
builds Linux-dependent sources; an unsupported local platform records NOTRUN,
not a substituted implementation. No guest image/build/qualification is run here.

Checkpoint A finishes the pure leaves and their two actual callers, ordinary
fixture tests and unchanged live acquisition paths. B completes genuine held
acquisition, fixed H/T/P carriers, all owner projections and complete actual
accounting. At the c1d9 design source, shared value owners exist but
evals-launcher.sh:309-328 stages review files only; evals-driver.sh:775-796 lacks
the malicious arm, and replay-malicious.py has neither production seed assembly
nor replay_case. They are required C work, not an existing API to reuse.
C completes fixed private seed assembly, malicious dispatch/coordinator,
run/dashboard staging, both sources, closure pins, regressions and restoration.
Its inactive source work may precede B acceptance through frozen real interfaces.
First independently accept and land this separate high-risk plan, normally merge
that accepted main into the same carrier, and obtain fresh exact-source preflight,
an exact claim and the manager's explicit resume GO before source work.
Bind each C consumer to its actual owner, fields, types, bounds, caller and return
values at a frozen checkpoint. Within the existing scope, scope P and SOURCE/D
interfaces may be completed first, then frozen and independently preflighted
before the C code that depends on them. Other C code with fully mapped real
interfaces need not wait for B acceptance. Unknown fields, missing owners or
dependencies on an unapproved T protocol keep the affected code blocked; never
invent an interface or use fixtures to supply a missing production dependency.

B's complete terminal, resource and cost proof, and all other original B proof,
remain hard gates for C acceptance, seeding, final G3 and implementation merge.
No runnable or full-CI milestone, or proof already due, is deferred. The changed
order accepts no part of B and grants no T terminal flag, file or exit-code
protocol, wider scope, budget or permission. Missing parent production source
still blocks its real hookup and full RAW16 qualification, not the defined
receiver/carrier/C mechanism after the source gates above. No checkpoint may
disguise missing whole B accounting as completed B. These are clean commits on
the same carrier, not partial implementation PRs or G3. A checkpoint cannot
claim complete source receipt while any required file is absent.

### Raw observations and capture wiring

The accepted spec is the sole schema definition for R4A.B. Construct its private
case v2 envelope with case-level incident/identities and the exact ordered
occurrences. Each entry has only role/binding/raw. Keep H/T/P, native and
completion schemas and bytes unchanged. The ten logical groups are distributed
across their original owners, not copied into nine empty or partial objects.
The table paths below are logical paths inside the spec's fixed containers.
No new implicit environment input is allowed.

The following historical line anchors bind source
`40022eb3b86c72b3d4d19b6e76cf21c68a41b371`; rebind by the named actual
caller edges to select observations before checking candidate role labels:

| Fixed occurrence / container | Existing caller and binding owner |
| --- | --- |
| reproduction.prepare, reproduction.inspect / preparation | enforced-reproduction.py:2394–2410 invokes and retains prepare, original cleanup, then inspect; `_preparation_capture_binding_values` checks each original T input/response/operation |
| reproduction / local, capture | enforced-reproduction.py:2424–2449 constructs and runs its launch; `_capture_for_replay` and `_launch_capture_values` bind the held H/P to that outer/launch/expectation/receipt |
| write.prepare, write.inspect / preparation | write-shadow.py:708–712 retains its two fixed operations from their separate scratch roots; use the same T binding owner independently |
| write / local, capture | write-shadow.py:725 calls `_verify_write`; the fixed writer kind selects its own launch relationship through the same consumer owners |
| gate / generator, scope_reader | generate-gate-evidence.py:349–354 creates its local reader/session; :523–525 calls gate_scope; consumer:3985–3995 invokes the existing evaluator; read-shadow-evidence.py:1503 creates the separate scope reader. Retain its original session/request/scope/store relation |
| kill / local, capture | generate-gate-evidence.py:438–492 constructs and binds the kill source; gate.generator owns its entropy; original held source request/expectation/receipt/package and capture owners validate the source |
| coverage / local, capture | read-shadow-evidence.py:1198–1224 constructs coverage only on its original branch; gate.scope_reader owns its entropy; retain that source's original held relationship checks |

The four preparation occurrences precede their own launches in wire order;
gate precedes kill and optional coverage. This is the spec's serialization order,
not a replacement execution schedule. Preserve gate setup, source calls, scope
branch and final origins/manifest/completion in the original native order.
Do not infer a missing observation from an expected output, another occurrence,
matching bytes or a held file with a plausible name. An unavailable genuine
container or original interface keeps its dependent assembly step blocked.

| Case group | R4A.B fields and occurrence | Acquisition → same owner proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| RAW01 | case incident.observed_at | Existing incident bytes → incident validator and record builders; mutate timestamp only |
| RAW02 | case identities.attempt_ids | Three actual caller ids → launch/write/gate refs; no nonce-derived id accepted |
| RAW03 | case identities.locators by fixed occurrence | Original role strings → identical derived paths/argv; never open these paths offline |
| RAW04 | launch local entropy.reproduction/write; gate generator/scope_reader entropy.kill/coverage | Original fresh read in that caller → request/call ids; one-byte mutation changes derived refs |
| RAW05 | each of four preparation containers' entropy.preparation_names | Original workspace entropy/collision observations → same bounded naming path; no live deterministic injection |
| RAW06 | each applicable container's clocks.wall/monotonic | Original owner sites/ordinals and bits → durations/deadlines/UTC; reject gaps/unused/NaN/Inf; never concatenate controllers or gate contexts |
| RAW07 | each local/preparation/gate container's storage.entries | Original held before/after/named stat, ACL, raw names/errors → same predicates; preserve every occurrence |
| RAW08 | each original owner's storage.identity_context | Real uid/gid/groups/resolver/platform → same ACL/ownership predicates; another context cannot substitute |
| RAW09 | each launch capture's guest.setup | Its own setup reads/returns → same enforcement rows; raw failure cannot become a hard flag |
| RAW10 | each launch capture's guest.control and launch | Its own launch at historical :1404/:1419 → own positive PID and waited==child; consume all closed tags in original order |
| RAW11 | each launch capture's guest.counters | Its own counter/statvfs/evidence bytes → report/frame, rounding, reached flags and sizes |
| RAW12 | each launch capture's host.process | Own six-key process/pipe/export/cleanup rows → stop, EOF, reap, frame/tail, timeout and cleanup; fixed listdir sites retain complete ordered name hex lists in return, not RAW07 or bytes; consume every original site/ordinal/cleanup level independently for each launch; export binding does not stand in for the other rows |
| RAW13 | each launch capture's host.driver_rest | Own zero-based host call indices and matching REST rows → driver argv/state/stop; no cross-launch mapping or socket offline |
| RAW14 | each source capture's source_pages.kill/coverage | Original family/page/cursor order → raw manifest, normalization, register and receipt |
| RAW15 | each applicable container's commands.runtime_values | Original qualified version/ABI/config facts → concrete argv/component identity; no copied component output |
| RAW16 | each original owner's runtime inventory | Original fixed H/T/P inventory shape, full bytes/locators/read failures → individual/composite identities; no executed slots, renamed files or merged occurrences |

Validate the spec's exact binding keys before projection. Launch bindings cover
all groups, including paths without export. Preserve control/subject/store and
outer/request/attempt/expectation/receipt checks. T bindings also retain the
original caller/input/response/operation and native cleanup/terminal seals.
Source bindings retain request SHA, attempt/id, gate refs, package/accepted-set,
expectation, receipt, capture and completion. Derive private operation only from
the fixed caller and original owner: kill-register-read or coverage-read. Reject
versioned aliases. The original kill request owner still validates
kill-register-read.v1; the original coverage receipt owner still validates
coverage-read.v1. Coverage request has no operation field; do not add one.

Require every mandatory occurrence once and no extra, duplicate, reordered or
unused entry. Coverage and its locator/entropy/pages/runtime/request/capture/
outputs are absent only when the same original gate calculation proves no call.
No null/empty entry or supplied absence flag replaces that proof. A failed
mandatory call cannot yield a shorter complete case. Within reached owners,
retain actual errors and independently reconstruct unreachable sites. Keep every
inner shape and allow empty arrays only where the original reached path allows.
Each row retains missing-value, extra-key, wrong-type and invalid/over-limit
negatives. Capture overflow keeps the old native cleanup/result semantics and
makes recording unusable; it never changes native success into replay success.

For every row save the actual caller location, value-constructor location, raw
case ID, occurrence/container and derived output destinations in the final source
receipt. Instantiate all 13 R4A.C output families for each applicable occurrence,
not just successful receipts. Keep original paths/roles for equal-named outputs;
rebuild original capture/completion/T documents before final comparison. Reject an unknown
raw/output inventory. Compare complete canonical documents and raw file bytes,
including every envelope/ref/hash, every source snapshot, manifest and COMPLETE.
Do not give expected output bytes to constructors to make equality tautological.

Keep the native attempt inventory exactly receipt.json/payload. Capture lives in
`.capture-<attempt_id>.pending`, then `.capture-<attempt_id>`, directly under the
same authenticated held store root. Only the original host principal writes it.
Use the spec's exact completed inventory: capture.json, complete.json, exactly
the fixed RAW16 runtime files named by its raw inventory, and conditional
sandbox-export.bin. Source-page captures contain their fixed package runtime
bytes but no export. Pending or old flat capture files never seed replay. The guest observation frame stays
outside verifier output; normal shadow bundles and fixed trial export paths do
not change. No real host installation or capture is authorized here.

After original request/store/attempt validation and claim, exclusively create the
host pending directory before measurement. Tee only bytes already returned by
original reads into fixed runtime files there. Preserve the genuine full holds
until required content checks finish; do not defer acquisition to publication.
Only after native receipt/payload, stop/reap and work-root cleanup finish does
`_capture_values` build and validate the entire LF-terminated envelope and its
runtime inventory. Overflow retains all early partials and cannot substitute an
empty raw group. `_write_capture_export` preserves its original bytes, read order,
exclusive create, 0440 owner/group, fsync and fused full readback/SHA. Use the same
private directory publication owner for launch and source-page captures.
Native syncs and cleanup remain unchanged. Sync the completed pending directory and store root, rename the directory without
replacement, then sync the published directory and store root. Only after these
succeed construct the spec's at-most-512-byte completion envelope in the existing
host value owner from exact request/capture bytes. Write .completion.pending
exclusively; finalize ownership/mode, fsync, close the write fd, hold/read back and
check its full bytes, identity and parents. Sync directory and store again, then
perform the final no-replace rename to complete.json. There is no post-commit
repair, retry, migration or extra status file. Keep all successful and partial
work charged and all actual failed bytes at their actual fixed location.

The completion name is the commit point, not a claim that its own rename is crash
durable. A failed or indeterminate final rename keeps its actual return/errno and
namespace outcome. No code infers rollback or retries it. The recorder requires
the one complete stable inventory; the witness only certifies earlier completed
checks. A failure before those gates or a missing/partial witness cannot seed.
Keep private failure/counter observations in the existing host owner; fixture raw
receipts retain actual return/errno even if a later close or observation also
fails. Do not add a persistence channel, diagnostic file or native output change
when the original failure prevents further recording.

In `_consumer.py`, restore read_store_attempt, read_coverage_attempt and the
native held recheck to their original receipt/payload closure. Add separate
private capture acquisition only inside the recorder failure boundary, after the
native receipt/expectation has passed. Reuse the same authenticated store hold;
open the fixed capture directory and exact files with existing _open_child,
_store_rule, HeldPath and ACL checks. Both launch callers still derive their role
from the frozen outer request. Bind completion, capture, request, receipt and
expectation to that same attempt/launch before projection. Source readers use
their actual source request/receipt and complete fixed source-package runtime
inventory, with no export role. A capture refusal marks
recording unusable and releases its holds; it must not escape as native E_STORE.
Do not suppress a native receipt, inventory, ACL, teardown or admission failure.

Keep native and capture snapshots separate. Origin/storage names identify their
actual distinct held directories; never pretend the binary or witness was in
the native attempt. Extend the existing origin value owner for these fixed names
only. It independently checks both closed inventories and hashes every file on
every original invocation. Consumer copy retains original capture and completion
bytes in its existing private recording inventory: add complete.json beside
capture.json in each fixed reproduction/write capture directory and each source
capture's existing directory. Carry that exact relative file through the existing
seed manifest and shared-file staging; retain the 1–256 shared-file bound. The
private witness slot has a fixed 512-byte ceiling, never a caller-supplied bound.
Offline rebuilds both through
the host's same value constructors, using independently reconstructed request and
capture bytes for that occurrence; it compares whole documents before final projection.
Do not use the case v2 wrapper as a native capture envelope. The witness
is never a guest input or a native success verdict. Update recursive scope/source
readers, held release/reacquisition and final checks together. Keep every old
independent SHA, equality and full export recheck; the directory move removes no
byte pass. Keep legacy native reads and reject old flat captures for seeding.

### RAW16 acquisition, carriers and parent frame

Implement this boundary only in the existing host, consumer, enforced/writer,
preparation, source and scope owners listed below. Extend secure_walk to retain
its genuine original ancestor chain through measurement; do not wrap a returned
target integer as HeldPath. The current parent Anchor has no full runtime map.
The test-only copy_runtime_slots helper and fixture ParentInputs cannot supply a
production source. Fixed roles come from original validated owner inputs, never
a generic collector, caller selector or historical locator reopen.

H/T/P are fixed owners of one closed private boundary, never caller-selected
transports. Derive names only from fixed role/index/operation. Retain real full
ancestor and target holds from original acquisition; an integer is not a
fabricated HeldPath. Record the original storage.entries observations and enforce
their predicates. Bind the two launches and kill/coverage source roles to their
own validated request, attempt, operation, qualification and completion.
Source shorthand is fixed: enforced/writer/consumer are the corresponding
shadow/v1 files; prep/preparation is preparation/v1/prepare-candidate.py;
scope Reader/reader is scope/v1/read-shadow-evidence.py; host is
sandbox/v1/host-supervisor.py. _NativeRelease belongs to enforced; Anchor and
capture_parent_context belong to consumer.

表中 H 是原 host-controlled capture，P 是既有 consumer private recorder，T 是 preparation 在原已验证 scratch 父根下的固定私有录制命名空间。H/P/T 是同一封闭 RAW16 carrier 合同的原有 owner 落点；不是三种可选 transport。每个生产者只写自己的原私有目录。只有 consumer 读已提交 H/T 并写 P。不得让 host 写 P，不增加目录型公共入口。

每个 row 都绑定 owner/site/原 acquisition 序号、固定 role/index、原 locator、原 request/attempt/operation、qualification 字节引用、原读取 errno 和完整 bytes。路径只能来自当前已校验的原 owner 配置或固定代码，不来自历史 capture。多次实际读取分别保留观察并计读取成本；不同字节必须保留独立 slot occurrence，不能用相同 role 合并。相同字节也不豁免实际物理副本计费。

coverage schema固定 default-branch-history/publisher-pull-requests/branch-rule-history；kill固定 repository-default-branch/first-parent-register-history/default-branch-ref-history/operator-merges。加config/accepted/package后，分别为8、9个package byte成员；host-config/supervisor/Python是上述独立角色。两套package角色与原owner精确schema一致，不按目录扫描。

preparation raw bytes只在producer_identity/copy_dependencies原读取期间存在；在原函数处tee到原 args._boundaries["scratch"] held父根下的固定T runtime文件，沿用T现有input/response/operation绑定。T从首次创建即与native scratch_work分开；不先写child再迁移，不让父consumer之后重开 /usr/bin/git。旧child-local capture.json不作runtime接口或回退来源。具体接线按下述T顺序实施。

**Parent fd3: two original records.**

capture_parent_context 的 _read_plain_fd(fd=3) 在解析前只读原schema2 JSON。原raw中有helper_build_record_sha256和native_release.dependency_record_sha256，没有对应bytes或可用的原holds。只保留这份raw不会补齐record。_NativeRelease另开的build-record.json只解决native build，不解决这两个record。

固定设计源中找到的原record持有者 ParentInputs 位于 scripts/test/shadow-enforced.test.sh:1011起的临时测试生成代码。它不属于真实parent生产实现。不得把它提升成生产资格证明，也不得以两个摘要去扫描文件、猜兄弟路径或重开历史locator。

唯一接口为同一fd3的v3封闭frame，不加fd或selector：20字节header（8字节固定magic `YSRAW16\0`，随后三个网络字节序uint32长度），依次是原schema2 context JSON、helper build record原bytes、native runtime dependency record原bytes。Context仍≤16,384 B；每个record仍≤1,048,576 B、原canonical schema不变。frame上限2,113,556 B，准确为20+16,384+2×1,048,576；拒绝短读、尾随字节、重复record、长度/digest/qualification关系不符。

原父方必须在它首次读取且真实持有这两个records时，把同一字节写入自己原有fd3 backing file。它先做原qualification，再检查前后真实chain/ACL/name/content并封口fd3；子方读同一fd3，不读取record locator。子方保留实际原frame和各payload，绑定frame/context SHA及后续request/attempt，再写P；不能只保留v3生成出来的一个摘要。原schema2可继续供原native路径，但不能产生完整RAW16 seed。

这改变的是公有fd3 wire/limit，不是改变原record内容、资格标准或native verdict。新增payload按runtime计，原context/metadata仍按原M及所有适用raw账计；不借payload分类挪走旧M费用。原parent已需写fd3 backing file，此接口不增加parent目的目录，但增加其内容/容量及retention范围。

**生产接线阻断精确为：固定设计源的产品入口链没有提供创建这份真实fd3的production调用源码。** 这是implementation-first硬依赖。本计划独立接受并合入、且manager完成精确source gate后，可在当前允许的_consumer.py实现封闭解帧及私有值构造，在现有测试写fixture；它们不能证明父方已调用。若完成该原调用者需要40 edit之外的harness文件，manager必须取得它的真实path/head及新allowlist决定。不能猜一个文件名，不能以测试函数当交付。真实parent接线、qualification和完整RAW16继续阻断；此缺源不阻止上述门后的有限inactive机制实施。

新增carrier streaming每块≤64 KiB。原native read_all/sha256_fd的1 MiB读取、read/EOF/errno语义保持；tee只切分已返回的同一块。小文件原raw尚存时可延迟到原私有目录可用后写，保持该raw及真实holds；大文件从原measurement逐块写，不增加完整大文件buffer。非host各owner使用自己的原read结果，不能由host补采。

源read前后、源与carrier内容复查前后均检查持有链、parent/name与fd身份、owner/mode/ACL及检查后的再fstat。新增完整源复查只影响recording，不覆盖native measured值。carrier排他创建；完整写、fsync、成功close writer、held readback、全字节比较和独立SHA均须成功。全体target在原native关闭点按原顺序关闭；新增ancestor/carrier holds在finally逆序逐个close，一次close失败仍关闭其余，不重试不确定fd。原native首错保持；recording首错和后续close/ledger错各保留，次错不能遮首错。

任一记录/资源/重检/close失败使录制不可用；native原退出、stdout零、receipt/payload及清理路径不改。所有pending、published、partial、旧失败留在实际位置，不删、不迁移、不重试。unknown费用不是零。completion不能修复缺bytes或失败检查。真实磁盘资源干扰仍需执行时资源授权，不能把控制流隔离说成物理无干扰。

### T preparation lifecycle implementation order

Use the spec's original held scratch-parent layout. These steps change only the
existing preparation/consumer/enforced/writer owners and their already allowed
tests. No new root, container, public parameter, inherited fd, loader, helper,
source member or fee transport. Do not copy a C renderer or broaden a module-origin
allowlist. The bound line numbers below are 25af design locations, not permission
to edit unreviewed WIP or proof that its T implementation is complete.

1. In `_consumer.py`, add the private fixed-name predicate used by its existing
   callers. Preparation constructs the same prescribed names in its current value
   owner. Define the reservation before any T create or native prepare/inspect
   dispatch; never derive it from C or a discovered directory's contents. The exact
   names are `raw16-t-v1-<op>-runtime-<role>-<index>.bin`,
   `raw16-t-v1-<op>-capture.json`, and
   `raw16-t-v1-<op>-complete-<sha256(C)>`. `op` is prepare/inspect, `role` is
   python/jq/git, index is 00..63 in actual acquisition order, and the digest is
   all 64 lowercase hex characters. This is the spec's closed grammar, not a
   generic prefix exemption. Existing case/window slot limits stay aggregate.
2. In preparation `parse_args/create_context` (2458–2498/2168–2203), retain the
   original validated scratch-parent chain and its fd. Keep native child creation,
   entropy, ctx.scratch.name, argv/env, candidate.git, sidecars and dependencies
   unchanged. Tee original Python/Git/jq acquisitions from producer_identity and
   copy_dependencies into exclusive flat T files through that parent fd. Maintain
   full source holds and original native close order; new failure affects recording
   eligibility only. Preserve the original C construction and its native-workspace
   check; add the complete fixed runtime inventory to that same closed document.
   SOURCE/script bytes stay in D's complete 84-member class. Prove actual script,
   selector, registry, protocol and generation bytes against the held D snapshots
   and original source/dependency identities. Keep every original read/hash,
   snapshot write/read/hash, after_data recheck and SOURCE/runtime overlap in Q
   and applicable existing ledgers. Never omit them or add a fourth T executable.
3. In `_write_preparation_observations`, complete runtime/C writes, writer close,
   source/carrier full readback, independent SHA/equality, chain/name/ACL checks,
   original required syncs and the T-parent sync, all new recording holds' actual
   successful closes and fallible accounting prerequisites before commit. Existing
   suppressing HeldBoundary.close is not evidence that a new close succeeded.
   Preserve first recording error plus later close/ledger errors with once-close;
   do not retry an uncertain fd. Then use only the original native scratch-parent
   fd for one exclusive 0700 mkdir of the complete-C SHA marker, including C's LF.
   Do not add another parent fd, temporary witness, second document or post-commit
   chmod/chown/close exemption. The borrowed fd keeps its original native lifetime.
   Keep actual mkdir return/errno and either uncertain namespace outcome without
   retry, rollback or inferring success from later presence. No crash durability
   of the marker name is claimed and no original sync is removed.
4. In `Anchor.retain_preparation` (1022–1087), acquire the fixed operation's flat
   capture/runtime/marker from the genuine held parent; remove the old child-local
   capture search as an acceptance route. Root enumeration remains at most 128
   total entries, including native children and both operations' T names. Do not
   give T a separate 128 or increase any file/metadata cap. Validate canonical full
   C, actual caller operation/input_raw/response_raw, current runtime inventory,
   unique matching empty stable marker, full bytes/hash/equality and final rechecks.
   Empty-marker proof uses enumeration, not nlink alone. Other-operation names are
   separately bound retained data or charged unusable occupancy, never this
   operation's seed. Extra/wrong/old/partial/unknown entries cannot create success.
   Only committed T may enter P. Any consumer acquire/release/close/ledger failure
   still makes recording unusable even when a valid marker exists.
5. At enforced reproduction's original calls and cleanup (2098–2106), keep the
   prepare call, retention call, intermediate cleanup and inspect call in order.
   Cleanup excludes only the predeclared exact T names; every other child follows
   the same rmtree-versus-unlink branch, timing and propagated errors. Native
   candidate-preparation children and all their contents are removed as before.
   A broad prefix, C-supplied name or unknown child never becomes a preservation
   whitelist. Retain T zero/partial/committed/failed occupancy in place regardless
   of a P copy or recording failure. In writer (690–694), keep the original
   preparation-scratch/inspection-scratch separation and retention call per actual
   operation. No new cleanup or automatic adoption of old child-local T; a preserved
   old attempt needs its separate disposition before this mechanism can reuse it.
6. Exercise the actual production caller path in the existing
   candidate-content-preparation, shadow-enforced and shadow-write test suites.
   A small ordinary prepare→cleanup→inspect fixture must preserve both complete T
   sets while removing native children at the original point; writer must preserve
   its two separate roots. Use real fixture files and genuine holds, full three-role
   acquisitions and original value functions; no hand-built slot map stands in for
   the production tee. Assert native argv/env/name/entropy, result, output and
   cleanup error behavior remain equal to the baseline. This is controlled proof,
   not real-runtime qualification. Use the RT.T vectors below and the existing
   private source-copy mutation route; no new test framework or entry point.

All names, files, empty directories, hash/canonical work, sync/enumeration/close
and failed/uncertain operations pay the unchanged original and RAW16 ledgers.
Preserve all carry-in, partials and failures without reset or refund. No actual
fit or complete M/Q proof follows from a location change. The original enforced
finally and writer Workspace.close close holds only; the external caller's final
disposition remains UNKNOWN. Real collection/retention/window still requires its
existing operator authority and a known compatible caller lifecycle. A permanent
root, new parent/interface/permission/budget, native-data retention exception,
partial deletion/migration or uncommitted reader requires that specific decision.
This plan grants none. No host or parent collects bytes for another owner.

### Private no-replace binding and platform proof

Use one private function at the two fixed host rename sites. It receives held
parent descriptors and internally derived basenames, never caller paths. Keep
ctypes in the existing host system boundary; no new library, helper executable,
source member, syscall-number table, public selector or capability probe is added.
Clear/read the saved errno around one call with use_errno=True. Preserve the
actual return/errno, including EIO/EINTR, without automatic retry. Missing symbol,
unsupported flag/filesystem, EXDEV, collision or any other error cannot fall back
to replacing rename, copy/delete or a precheck-then-rename sequence.

| Platform | Fixed binding and fact gate |
| --- | --- |
| Darwin | Existing _darwin_libc system library; renameatx_np has argtypes [c_int,c_char_p,c_int,c_char_p,c_uint], restype c_int. Pass source held directory fd, source basename bytes, destination held directory fd, destination basename bytes, flags RENAME_EXCL=0x00000004 only. Local SDK sys/stdio.h:37,53 provides the constant/prototype; sys/attr.h:360–364 identifies filesystem-dependent exclusive rename/open-file support. Header declarations do not prove runtime/filesystem support. |
| Linux | Intended libc renameat2 no-replace boundary. Exact local Linux prototype, flag definition and symbol availability are UNKNOWN in this author's macOS evidence. Do not implement guessed argtypes, flag value or syscall number. First bind the primary declarations on the existing Linux CI runner as described below; use only those verified types/constant in the same private ctypes owner. No raw-syscall fallback. |

After this plan's protected landing and fresh source GO, add one bounded fact step
to existing sandbox-launcher.test.sh. On Linux use its existing C compiler to
preprocess the fixed installed stdio.h with _GNU_SOURCE and linux/fs.h; retain
header paths/bytes hashes, the complete renameat2 declaration and
RENAME_NOREPLACE definition. Compile only a tiny temporary translation unit that
includes those headers and refers to that declaration/constant; no executable,
link/run, syscall or filesystem probe in this fact step. Preserve full compiler
argv/stdout/stderr/exit. Missing headers/declaration/constant stops the Linux
binding; never install or fetch them. Publish a clean same-carrier source
checkpoint only through the manager's existing review/GO/CI path below. The
manager reads the exact-head raw facts before Linux production binding is coded.
Darwin/shared owners and accounting may proceed independently. This fact step
neither skips existing assertions nor declares B complete.

After the verified binding exists, existing ordinary temp-store fixtures exercise
its real no-replace calls for both directory and completion-file publication.
Prove success and collision with both destination bytes/inodes unchanged; retain
return/errno and before/after names on errors. Both platforms need their ordinary
positive, not permanent unsupported refusal. These temporary-directory mechanism
checks grant no native sandbox qualification, real-store execution or platform
acceptance. Preserve the original genuine macOS/Linux gates and actual NOTRUN.

Original line anchors below bind immutable 64dda8e; capture publication and
consumer updates also bind cd5c4e8 host write_receipt_file/_write_capture_file/
_write_capture_export and consumer _origin_values/read_store_attempt/
_HeldStoreRead/read_coverage_attempt. Rebind final locations by function and hash;
no old line number or source checkpoint is a current resume tuple.
Use only the original 40 edit paths, sole C2 bootstrap exec and the separate
R9.2 qualifier compatibility exception;
all nine adoption-only paths and the exact 84-member source closure remain
unchanged. Rebind final line/blob/hash receipts.
